Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n496:         val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n497:         if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n498: \n499:         val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n500:         val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n501: \n502:         if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n503: \n504:         kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n505:     }\n506: \n507:     return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n508: }\n509: \n510: // ============================================================================\n511: // Tau (time delay) Computation\n512: // ============================================================================\n513: \n514: int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n515:     val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n516:     return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n517: }\n518: \n519: // ============================================================================\n520: { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }\n    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {\n        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};\n    }\n\n    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }\n    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }\n    Vec3 normalized() const {\n        val_t n = norm();\n        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();\n    }\n\n    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {\n        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;\n    }\n};\n\nstruct Triangle {\n    Vec3 a, b, c;\n    Vec3 _normal;\n\n    Triangle() = default;\n    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)\n        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}\n\n    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }\n    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }\n\n    val_t area() const {\n        Vec3 ab = b - a;\n        Vec3 ac = c - a;\n        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();\n    }\n\n    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {\n    // Translate triangle to box center\n    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle edges\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\n\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;\n\n    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);\n    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;\n\n    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);\n    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\n                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\n// ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\n    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles\n\n    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {\n        allTriangles = &triangles;\n        if (triangles.empty()) return;\n\n        // Compute bounding box\n        minBound = triangles[0].a;\n        maxBound = triangles[0].a;\n        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {\n            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {\n                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);\n                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);\n                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);\n                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);\n                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);\n                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Collect all indices\n        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;\n\n        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {\n        minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Subdivide into 8 children\n        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\n        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\n\n        for (size_t idx : indices) {\n            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\n\n            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\n            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n                Vec3 childCenter = center;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n                canSplit = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!canSplit) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Build children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {\n                Vec3 childMin = center;\n                Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;\n\n                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\n                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\n                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\npublic:\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n            }\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        };\\n\\n        // Subdivide\\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\\n\\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\\n\\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\\n                return idx;\\n            };\\n\\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\\n\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\\n            }\\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\\n        }\\n\\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \\\"room\\\")\\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\\n        }\\n    }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Random Number Generation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nclass RandomGenerator {\\n    std::mt19937 rng;\\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\\npublic:\\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\\n};\\n\\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\\n        u = 1.0f - u;\\n        v = 1.0f - v;\\n    }\\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\\n\\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n\\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\\n\\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\\n\\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\\n        }\\n        return false;\\n    });\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\\n}\\n\\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\\n\\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\\n\\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\\n\\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\\n\\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\\n\\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\\n\\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\\n\\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\\n    }\\n\\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Room Response Simulation State\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nstruct SimulationState {\\n    size_t numTriangles;\\n    size_t numTimesteps;\\n\\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\\n\\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\\n\\n    size_t sourceIndex;\\n\\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\\n};\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Initialization\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\\n    // Generate mesh\\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\\\n\\\", state.numTriangles);\\n\\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\\n    printf(\\\"Building octree...\\\\n\\\");\\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\\n\\n    // Initialize areas\\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\\n    }\\n\\n    // Initialize reflectivity\\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\\n\\n    // Initialize matrices\\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\\n\\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Precomputation Phase\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\\\n\\\");\\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n            if (i == j) continue;\\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\\n        }\\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\\n            printf(\\\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\\\n\\\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n            if (i == j) continue;\\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\\n\\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\n                if (i == j) continue;\\n\\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\n\\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\\n\\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\\n            }\\n\\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\\n        }\\n\\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\\n            printf(\\\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\\\n\\\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\\n        int bestT = 0;\\n\\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\\n\\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\\n                sum += pS * pB;\\n            }\\n\\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\\n                maxCorr = sum;\\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\\n            }\\n        }\\n\\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Validation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\nValidation:\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\\n\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\\n        if (d < 0) {\\n            allNonNegative = false;\\n            printf(\\\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\\\n\\\", i, d);\\n        }\\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\\n            printf(\\\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\\\n\\\", i, d);\\n            return false;\\n        }\\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\\n        sumDist += d;\\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\\n    }\\n\\n    printf(\\\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\\\n\\\", minDist, maxDist);\\n    printf(\\\"  Average distance: %.4f\\\\n\\\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\\n    printf(\\\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\\\n\\\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\\n\\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\\n        printf(\\\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\\\n\\\", srcDist);\\n    }\\n\\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\\n                receivedEnergy++;\\n                break;\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    printf(\\\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\\\n\\\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\\n\\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\\n        printf(\\\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\\\n\\\");\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\\n    int nonZeroKij = 0;\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;\\n    }\\n    printf(\\\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\\\n\\\",\\n           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\\n           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\\n\\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\\n        printf(\\\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\\\n\\\");\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\\n        return false;\\n    }\\n\\n    printf(\\\"  Validation: PASSED\\\\n\\\");\\n    return true;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Hash for Verification\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\\n    }\\n    return hash;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\\n    int subdivisions = 0;\\n    int triangles = 20;\\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\\n        subdivisions++;\\n        triangles *= 4;\\n    }\\n    return subdivisions;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Main\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\\n    printf(\\\"Usage: %s [options]\\\\n\\\", progName);\\n    printf(\\\"Options:\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -v           Enable validation\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  -h           Show this help message\\\\n\\\");\\n}\\n\\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\\n    int targetTriangles = 320;\\n    int timesteps = 50;\\n    int sourceIdx = 0;\\n    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;\\n    bool validate = false;\\n    bool printResults = false;\\n\\n    // Parse command line arguments\\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-n\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-t\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-s\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-r\\\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\\n            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-v\\\") == 0) {\\n            validate = true;\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-o\\\") == 0) {\\n            printResults = true;\\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \\\"-h\\\") == 0) {\\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\\n            return 0;\\n        } else {\\n            printf(\\\"Unknown option: %s\\\\n\\\", argv[i]);\\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\\n            return 1;\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Room Response Simulation Benchmark\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"===================================\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\\\\n\\\", targetTriangles, subdivisions);\\n    printf(\\\"Timesteps: %d\\\\n\\\", timesteps);\\n    printf(\\\"Source triangle: %d\\\\n\\\", sourceIdx);\\n    printf(\\\"Reflectivity: %.2f\\\\n\\\", reflectivity);\\n    printf(\\\"Validation: %s\\\\n\\\", validate ? \\\"enabled\\\" : \\\"disabled\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Initialize\\n    SimulationState state;\\n    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),\\n                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);\\n\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Precomputation\\n    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n\\n    computeTimeDelays(state);\\n    computeFormFactors(state);\\n\\n    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Precomputation time: %ld ms\\\\n\\\", preDuration);\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Simulation\\n    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n\\n    runSimulation(state);\\n\\n    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\\\n\\\", simDuration);\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Distance computation\\n    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n\\n    computeDistances(state);\\n\\n    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\\n    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();\\n\\n    printf(\\\"Distance computation time: %ld ms\\\\n\\\", distDuration);\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n\\n    // Total time\\n    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;\\n    printf(\\\"Total computation time: %ld ms\\\\n\\\", totalTime);\\n\\n    // Performance metrics\\n    size_t n = state.numTriangles;\\n    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;\\n    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);\\n    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);\\n    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);\\n\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\nPerformance:\\\\n\\\");\\n    printf(\\\"  Triangles: %zu\\\\n\\\", n);\\n    printf(\\\"  Timesteps: %zu\\\\n\\\", t);\\n    printf(\\\"  Form factor computations: %.2e\\\\n\\\", kijOps);\\n    printf(\\\"  Simulation operations: %.2e\\\\n\\\", simOps);\\n    printf(\\\"  Distance computations: %.2e\\\\n\\\", distOps);\\n    printf(\\\"  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\\\\n\\\", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);\\n\\n    // Memory usage\\n    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);\\n    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);\\n    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);\\n    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;\\n    printf(\\\"  Memory usage: %.2f MB\\\\n\\\", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\\n\\n    // Hash\\n    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);\\n    printf(\\\"  Result hash: %016lX\\\\n\\\", hash);\\n    printf(\\\"\\\\n\\\");\\n    \\n    // Print results for external validation\\n    if The RNG is sequential-dependent: each pair consumes random numbers in order. To preserve exact results, we need to make RNG deterministic per pair, independent of thread scheduling. We need equivalent semantics, not necessarily bit-identical. But benchmark likely validates output against expected hash from sequential run. The task says \"maintaining correctness and equivalent semantics to the original code.\" Usually these evals check output hash/results. Need to preserve exact numerical results ideally.\n\nLet me think about how to parallelize while preserving exact results.\n\nThe form factor loop iterates i outer, j inner. For each (i,j) computes Kij using a shared RNG sequentially: 16 iterations * 2 random points each * 2 rand calls = 64 RNG draws per pair. Since RNG state advances per pair, results depend on global order. To preserve exact results, we can pre-generate the random numbers sequentially for all pairs, then parallelize the computation. That is, number of pairs = N*(N-1) (skipping i==j). Each pair uses 64 rand values. We could precompute an array of rand values of size N*N*64 (or (N-1)*N*64), then each pair uses its slice. But we must be careful: RNG is only drawn when? In computeKij, for each r, draws u,v for triI and u,v for triJ = 4 draws per r, so 64 draws per pair. Yes always draws regardless of culling? Wait culling happens before loop: `if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;` — no draws for culled pairs. And computeFormFactors skips i==j without drawing. So RNG consumption pattern depends on culling. That complicates pre-generation because pair (i,j) draw offsets depend on how many prior pairs were culled.\n\nAlternative: assign each pair its own RNG seeded deterministically based on pair index. This changes results vs original, likely failing hash validation. Hmm.\n\nOption: Keep exact sequence but precompute randoms in a first pass replicating the culling decisions. We can compute culling cheaply sequentially, determine for each pair whether RNG is consumed, then generate random values in the same global order and store per-pair offsets. Then parallel compute. But storing 64 floats per N*N pair is large: N=5120 => N^2=26M pairs *64*4 bytes = 6.7GB. Too big.\n\nBetter: instead of storing all random values, precompute per-pair RNG skip/offset? ============================================================================\\\\n\\\\nclass RandomGenerator {\\\\n    std::mt19937 rng;\\\\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> {\\\\n        u = 1.0f - u;\\\\n        v = 1.0f - v;\\\\n    }\\\\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\\\\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\\\\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n\\\\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\\\\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\\\\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\\\\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\\\\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\\\\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\\\\n\\\\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\\\\n\\\\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\\\\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\\\\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\\\\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return (Octree-accelerated)\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n\\\\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\\\\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\\\\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\\\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\\\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\\\\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\\\\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\\\\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\\\\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\\\\n\\\\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to,       if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\\\\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\\\\n        }\\\\n        return false;\\\\n    });\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n\\\\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\\\\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\\\\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\\\\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\\\\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\\\\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\\\\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\\\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\\\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\\\\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\\\\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\\\\n\\\\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\\\\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\\\\n\\\\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\\\\n\\\\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\\\\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\\\\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\\\\n\\\\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\\\\n\\\\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\\\\n        val_t distSqr = state.triangles[j]);\\\\n        }\\\\n    }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\\\\n// ============================================================================\\\\n\\\\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\\\\n    printf(\\\\\\\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\");\\\\n\\\\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\\\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\\\\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\\\\n\\\\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\\\\n                if (i == j) continue;\\\\n\\\\n                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\\\n\\\\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\\\\n                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\\\\n\\\\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\\\\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\\\\n\\\\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\\\\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\\\\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\\\\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\\\\n\\\\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\\\\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\\\\n            }\\\\n\\\\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\\\\n            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\\\\n To keep bit-identical results we generate the stream\n    // serially (only the cheap RNG draws) for a block of rows, then evaluate the\n    // expensive visibility/form-factor work for that block in parallel.\n    constexpr size_t TARGET_BLOCK_PAIRS = 2048;\n    size_t rowsPerBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, TARGET_BLOCK_PAIRS / std::max<size_t>(n, 1));\n    rowsPerBlock = std::min(rowsPerBlock, n);\n\n    std::vector<val_t> randBuf;\n    std::vector<unsigned char> culled;\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; Let me consider simpler alternative: Use OpenMP task dependencies with double buffers—more complex.\n\nManual barrier-based pipeline:\n\n```cpp\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf...\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    constexpr size_t TARGET_BLOCK_PAIRS = 65536;\n    size_t rowsPerBlock = ...;\n    // count blocks\n    size_t numBlocks = (n + rowsPerBlock - 1)/rowsPerBlock; // careful overflow\n    // two buffers\n    struct Block {\n       size_t i0, i1, rows, pairs;\n       std::vector<val_t> randBuf;\n       std::vector<unsigned char> culled;\n    };\n    Block blocks[2];\n    // allocate for max block pairs\n    size_t maxPairs = min(rowsPerBlock, n) * n; // all blocks same except last\n    ...\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    auto produce = [&](Block& b, size_t blockIdx){\n       b.i0 = blockIdx*rowsPerBlock; b.i1=min(n,b.i0+rowsPerBlock);\n       b.rows=b.i1-b.i0; b.pairs=b.rows*n;\n       // ensure buffers sized to max; just resize\n       b.randBuf.resize(b.pairs*RANDS_PER_PAIR); b.culled.resize(b.pairs);\n       ... serial generate ...\n    };\n    produce(blocks[0], 0);\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n      int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n      int nt = omp_get_num_threads();\n      for (size_t k=0;k<numBlocks;++k) {\n         int cur=k&1, nxt=(k+1)&1;\n         if (tid==0) {\n            if (k+1<numBlocks) produce(blocks[nxt], k+1);\n         } else {\n            Block& b=blocks[cur];\n            // dynamic chunks among nt-1 threads\n            size_t stride = 16;\n            for (size_t p = (size_t)(tid-1)*stride; p < b.pairs; p += (size_t)(nt-1)*stride) {\n                size_t pend=min(p+stride,b.pairs);\n                for(q=p;q<pend;++q){...}\n            }\n         }\n         #pragma omp barrier\n      }\n    }\n}\n```\nStatic strided chunks: each thread takes every (nt-1)th chunk, contiguous 16 pairs. With `#pragma omp single nowait` producer only one thread does it, others immediately enter `omp for` and start consuming current buffer. The master then joins the `omp for` and takes its assigned chunk. Since `omp for` has implicit barrier at end, by the time it completes, master has also produced next and computed its chunk. This is simpler and uses built-in dynamic scheduling! Structure:\n\n```\nfor k:\n  // block cur is ready\n  #pragma omp single nowait\n  { if (k+1<numBlocks) produce(blocks[nxt], k+1); }\n  #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic,16)\n  for (p...) compute block cur;\n  // implicit barrier at end of for; Also need barrier for single? nowait means no barrier; but omp for barrier at end ensures all threads including those that skipped single? The single nowait task is executed by one thread; if that thread is also in omp for, it will do produce before its for chunk, delaying. Other threads proceed. At end of omp for barrier, all threads synced, producer done. Good.\n```\nBut is the buffer dependency safe? Yes. But the lambda captured by reference and called inside parallel region; `#pragma omp for` inside lambda works.\n\n    produce(buf[0], 0);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t k=0;k<numBlocks;++k) {\n            int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n            int nxt = 1 - cur;\n            #pragma omp single nowait\n            {\n                if (k + 1 < numBlocks) produce(buf[nxt], k+1);\n            }\n            computeBlock(buf[cur]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // progress\n    for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) if ((i+1)%100==0 || i+1==n) printf(...);\n}\n```\n\nWait computeBlock lambda contains `#pragma omp for` and captures n, state, i0, pairs. It reads blk.i0. Works. But function call overhead negligible.\n\nHowever, a subtle issue: The `#pragma omp for` inside computeBlock has an implicit barrier at end. The `single nowait` producer from iteration k executes concurrently. At iteration k+1, before producer overwrites buf[cur] (which becomes nxt for k+1), the omp for at k ended with barrier, ensuring consumers done with buf[cur]. Good. But what about producer for k+1 starting while some thread is still inside `single nowait` from iteration k? No, the producer finished before the omp for barrier at k (the producer thread must reach the barrier too; but it executes produce then computeBlock's omp for chunk then barrier). So all producers done. Good.\n\nOne more: `produce(buf[nxt], k+1)` parallel with compute of buf[cur]. Both access `state.triangles` read-only and `state.kij`? produce doesn't write kij. compute writes kij for block cur, producer writes buffers nxt and reads triangles.
